Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [135] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip是一种无需评估的无损技能压缩方法，通过最小描述长度（MDL）目标将自我进化智能体的技能压缩为最短的忠实结构解释，包含一次性模式和持续Zip-on-Write模式，在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化智能体的技能在积累过程中会重复记录相同的规则、复制相似的操作序列，导致技能变得冗长、注入成本高且难以维护。通用的提示压缩方法不适用于此场景，因为技能不是扁平文本，而是包含名称描述、工作流、工具/输出契约和异常规则的结构化实体。现有的评估引导压缩方法引入rollout、成本和评估集依赖。

Method: SkillZip将技能压缩形式化为带类型的MDL目标，在技能契约和残差之间分配，并施加硬覆盖约束（覆盖每个触发的规则、工作流边、工具要求、义务和输出字段）。核心直觉是"解释一次，多次引用"：重复规则在适用作用域声明一次，重复动作序列分解为共享过程，仅保留差异作为显式异常。提供简单共享阈值、天然保留唯一稀有规则、支持高效局部更新。包含一次性模式（单次结构化抽取调用+确定性优化）和持续的Zip-on-Write模式（整合自我进化补丁而无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史）。

Result: 通过全面实验评估，SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销方面展现了有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip提供了一种无需评估、无需rollout的技能压缩方案，将技能压缩视为寻找最短忠实结构解释的优化问题，兼顾了压缩率、泛化能力和低开销，适合自我进化智能体的持续技能管理。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [136] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个铁路交通站点知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，该数据集显式建模城市实体间的空间和语义交互，以支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流量预测、站点周边商店推荐）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路交通系统对城市流动性和经济发展至关重要，站点作为关键交通枢纽连接城市各处。然而，城市级轨道交通站点相关任务需要大规模城市数据，现有研究在数据组织上往往忽视各类城市实体之间的复杂交互关系。

Method: 构建RTSKG数据集，集成异构城市实体（如轨道交通站点、道路路段、兴趣点POI），设计统一的模式（schema），以Linked Data形式发布，可在线访问。

Result: 在站点周边商店推荐和知识增强的客流量预测两项任务上进行了评估，结果表明RTSKG能有效支持城市级轨道交通站点分析。

Conclusion: RTSKG通过显式建模城市实体间的空间与语义交互，为城市级轨道交通站点相关任务提供了有价值的数据资源，展示了其支持站点分析的潜力。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [137] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Agentic编码提示词（如CLAUDE.md）中的"灾难性记忆"现象——提示词随仓库演化无限增长，平均增长226%，每提交增加4.9条指令。通过反转IFEval基准，作者证明提示词注释（prompt comments）可消除99.3%的冗余指令，并将真实世界agentic指令跟随性能提升最多23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic编码提示词（如CLAUDE.md）在真实仓库中无限增长，只有仓库退役或有人重写文件才会停止。作者将此归因于"不完美回忆"：追加指令成本低，但一旦指令的推理依据丢失，删除它而不冒正确性回归的风险需要O(2^|D|)的代价。作者将其命名为"灾难性记忆"（catastrophic remembering），是持续学习中"灾难性遗忘"（catastrophic forgetting）的镜像现象。

Method: 1) 在247,694条指令生命周期、1,867个仓库上刻画灾难性记忆现象，分析指令增长率和删除风险。2) 反转IFEval基准，构造可验证世界（verifiable worlds），在其中最优提示词已知，通过添加编码潜在推理的注释来消除冗余指令。3) 将同样的反转方法应用于WildIFEval，验证注释在真实世界agentic指令跟随中的效果。

Result: 1) Agentic提示词无界增长，生命周期内增长+226%，每提交净增+4.9条指令；指令越老越不易被删除（log-hazard -0.032/提交）。2) 提示词注释可消除99.3%的冗余指令（从+211.3%降至+1.4%）。3) 在WildIFEval上，提示词注释可将真实世界agentic指令跟随性能提升最多23.1%。

Conclusion: 提示词注释是遏制灾难性记忆的有效机制：通过编码指令背后的推理依据，使删除冗余指令的成本从指数级降低到可操作水平。作者以"如果英语是新的代码，为什么还没有注释？"作为总结，呼吁将代码注释的实践范式引入Agentic提示词管理。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [138] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出sLTN，一种扩展的逻辑张量网络框架，将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为语言的一等公民引入，使LTN能够显式建模时序、顺序和图结构关系，并以原LTN语义为特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架主要适用于平坦集合形式的数据，无法显式捕捉时间顺序、序列位置或图连通性等结构组织信息，这限制了LTN在时序推理和关系推理等场景中的应用。

Method: 将结构维度作为命名张量轴引入LTN语言中，形式化定义了sLTN的语法和模糊张量语义；结构维度可被量化、通过结构关系关联，并直接在逻辑层面表达时序、序列和关系约束。提供了基于PyTorch的声明式签名、公式解析和张量解释的实现。

Result: 证明在无结构维度时sLTN语义可退化恢复为原始LTN语义作为特例；在代表性时序和序列推理示例上进行了说明性验证，并发布了配套的sltn开源库。

Conclusion: sLTN通过结构维度的一等化设计，使神经符号推理框架能够自然处理结构化的数据组织，扩展了LTN在时序和关系推理方面的表达能力，同时保持与原框架的兼容性。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [139] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个深入案例研究（改进Grothendieck常数K_G的上下界）展示了AI在数学研究中的有效性，并详细讨论了其优势、劣势以及创造理想条件促使AI产生突破性见解的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体日益被用于数学研究，但如何有效使用它们仍然不清晰，缺乏系统性的经验总结。

Method: 对AI改进Grothendieck常数K_G界的问题进行广泛案例研究，利用AI研究系统将已知最佳界收紧至6π/11 ≤ K_G ≤ π/(2log(1+√2)) - 10^-4，并详细记录使用体验。

Result: 成功将Grothendieck常数的上下界收紧，并产生了被领域专家认为具有新颖性的洞察；同时对AI在数学研究中的优缺点及创造理想条件的方法进行了系统总结。

Conclusion: AI在数学研究中可以产生突破性见解，其有效运用需要创造理想条件，并理解其优势与局限，为后续AI辅助数学研究提供实践指导。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [World-First SEM-based Recovery of Crash EDR Data from the EEPROM of a Severely Damaged SRS Module Using CrashScan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10152)
*Sergei Skorobogatov,Peter Vertal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种在汽车安全气囊（SRS）控制模块的数据存储芯片永久损坏后，仍能成功恢复数据的可行且经济实惠的方法。在真实事故中，处理器和Flash EEPROM芯片均遭受严重电损伤（芯片封装上有可见孔洞），但通过创新的样品制备和成像技术，仍以100%成功率提取了全部关键数据，随后使用标准EDR提取工具完成了完整的事故重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严重事故中，安全气囊控制模块的存储芯片往往因过电压和高电流而损坏，此前在存储器芯片损坏的情况下，没有任何可行或经济实惠的方案来完整提取或恢复数据。这严重阻碍了交通事故的法医重建工作。

Method: 采用创新的专有样品制备和成像技术，从物理损坏的Flash EEPROM芯片中提取数据。通过深入分析失效机制（过电压导致高电流流过硅片，局部温度超过3000°C，内部金属引线熔化蒸发，硅基底部分蒸发并被熔化的键合金丝填充），确认了损伤范围，但仍成功提取了位于损坏区仅几百微米外的完整EEPROM阵列数据。随后使用Collision Sciences CrashScan对恢复数据的捐赠单元进行标准EDR提取。

Result: 从真实严重事故中电气损坏（芯片封装可见孔洞）的SRS模块中以100%成功率提取了全部关键信息。尽管EEPROM阵列距损坏区域仅几百微米，但未丢失任何单个比特的数据。使用捐赠单元结合标准EDR提取工具成功生成了完整的事故重建报告。

Conclusion: 该方案证明了在永久性机械、电气或火灾损伤后恢复芯片数据的可行性，不仅在汽车领域具有重大法医价值，还可推广至医疗、航空和航天等领域。

Abstract: This paper introduces fully working and affordable approach to data recovery from automotive SRS (airbag control) module after permanent failure of its data storage memory chip. All essential information was successfully extracted with 100% success rate from a real SRS module after severe accident which resulted in both its Processor and Flash EEPROM chip electrically damaged with visible holes in their packages. Further investigation revealed that the damage was likely caused by overvoltage and subsequent high-current-flow through silicon die of chips. This not only caused some internal metal wires to melt and evaporate but also caused partial evaporation of silicon substrate and the created cavity was filled with melted gold from a bonding wire. This reveals the nature of the failure with local temperatures exceeding 3000°C. Despite to the fact that the on-chip EEPROM array was only a few hundred micrometers away from the damaged area, it did not lose any single bit of information. This proved the approach to be resilient for successful data recovery from severely damaged devices in the future.
  With the donor unit containing the recovered data, standard bench-top EDR extraction was performed using Collision Sciences CrashScan, yielding a full EDR report. The forensic significance of the recovered data, the technical novelty of the methodology, and the complete accident reconstruction are presented and discussed.
  Until now, if because of an accident the memory chip was damaged, there was no feasible or affordable solution for full data extraction or recovery. With the use of innovative and proprietary sample preparation and imaging techniques, it became possible to extract and verify all the data stored inside chips after permanent mechanical, electrical or fire damage. This could help not only automotive but also medical, aviation and aerospace industries.

</details>


### [141] [MarkNull: Model-Agnostic Watermark Removal in AI-Generated Images via On-Manifold Latent Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10166)
*Jie Cao,Qi Li,Zelin Zhang,Xiaodong Wu,Lingshuang Liu,Xiangman Li,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MarkNull，一种模型无关的水印移除攻击方法，通过流形内隐变量操纵来去相关水印图像中隐表示与嵌入噪声之间的统计依赖；还提出其摊销变体MarkNull-A，可将攻击蒸馏为单次前向推理，实现0.50秒/图的高效移除。实验表明该方法能将位准确率降至53.14%（接近随机猜测50%），且不产生可感知的图像退化，成功攻破Google SynthID-Image系统并有效迁移至视频水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对AI生成图像的数字水印移除攻击存在两大局限：一是只能攻击特定生成模型，缺乏普适性；二是移除水印时往往伴随严重的视觉退化。因此，亟需一种模型无关、同时保持高视觉质量的鲁棒性攻击方法，以真正检验水印系统在现实场景下的安全性。

Method: MarkNull的核心建立在关键观察之上：含水印图像在生成的隐表示与嵌入的初始噪声之间存在强统计依赖。基于此，作者引入Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS)量化该依赖，并构建优化目标，选择性地将隐表示与嵌入水印去相关，同时保持语义保真度。MarkNull-A则通过蒸馏将优化过程压缩为单次前向推理，实现近实时的水印移除。

Result: 在post-hoc、微调式和初始噪声式三类水印范式的广泛评估中，MarkNull将平均位准确率降至53.14%，接近随机猜测水平（50%），且无可感知图像退化。MarkNull-A实现0.50秒/图的推理速度，计算开销低。攻击成功突破Google SynthID-Image系统，并有效迁移至视频水印场景。作者还提出了相应的攻击检测机制作为防御对策。

Conclusion: MarkNull系列攻击揭示了现有水印系统在面对模型无关隐空间攻击时的脆弱性，证明仅依赖位准确率或视觉质量作为鲁棒性指标不足以反映真实威胁。该工作强调设计对隐空间操纵具有内在韧性的水印方案的必要性，并为水印鲁棒性评估提供了新的基准与思考方向。

Abstract: Digital watermarking has emerged as a critical technique for provenance and copyright attribution in AI-generated imagery, yet its robustness against realistic, model-agnostic removal attacks remains poorly explored. Existing attacks either succeed only against specific generative models or achieve removal at the cost of severe visual degradation. In this paper, we propose MarkNull, a model-agnostic watermark removal attack via on-manifold latent manipulation. MarkNull is grounded in a key observation: watermarked images exhibit a strong statistical dependency between the generated latent representation and the embedded initial noise. To quantify this dependency, we introduce the Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS) and formulate an optimization objective that selectively decorrelates the latent representation from the embedded watermark while preserving semantic fidelity. Extensive evaluations across different categories of watermarking paradigms, including post-hoc, fine-tuning-based, and initial-noise-based schemes, demonstrate that MarkNull reduces average bit accuracy to 53.14%, approaching random-guessing (50%), without perceptible image degradation. To further improve scalability, we propose MarkNull-A, an amortized, optimization-free variant that distills the attack into a single forward pass, achieving 0.50 s/image with modest computational overhead. Notably, our attacks successfully compromise Google's SynthID-Image system while preserving high visual quality and transfer effectively to video watermarking. Finally, we present an attack detection mechanism as a defensive counterpart to MarkNull and MarkNull-A, highlighting the necessity of developing watermark designs resilient to model-agnostic latent-space attacks.

</details>


### [142] [Withholding the Completing Chunk: Deterministic Pair-Completion Guardrails for Streaming LLM Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10279)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究流式语言模型输出中的一个发布时序问题：完整响应的审核发生在文本流出之后，而反复对部分文本做语义分类既昂贵又不稳定。作者构建了一种窄确定性的发布时机关卡：每个危险签名为两个词汇谓词的合取（conjunction），守卫在每次释放前扫描累积前缀，并扣留第一块使两个谓词都可观察的分块。在4个签名族、8种分块大小和32次机制试验中，流式决策与缓冲扫描器一致并扣留了所有补全对的分块；8个单谓词对照均通过。在512次策略对比试验中，全前缀扫描和完整缓冲检测到所有配置对，512字符窗口检测到96/128，块局部扫描检测到38/128。固定对标记了0/338个人类安全响应，检出0/394个陪审团标记的不安全响应，确认其覆盖范围窄而非普适。校准的Llama Guard 3 1B基线将310/338安全响应分类为安全、202/394不安全响应分类为不安全。16,384字符响应上重复前缀扫描器时间约13.261 ms至829.640 ms。结论：对完成配对是对小固定策略的精确发布边界兜底机制，而非语义审核的替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式LLM输出存在发布时序矛盾：完整响应审核发生在文本已流出之后，失去了阻止意义；而对部分文本反复做语义分类则成本高、结果不稳定。需要一个确定性、轻量且在流式发布前工作的机制。

Method: 构造一个确定性发布时机关卡（guard）：每个危险签名是少量固定词汇对（两个词汇谓词的合取）。守卫在每次释放前扫描累积前缀，当且仅当某分块使两个谓词同时可观察时扣留该分块。采用固定对（fixed pairs）而非语义分类器。

Result: 在4签名族、8种块大小、32次机制试验中，流式决策与缓冲扫描器完全一致且扣留所有补全对的分块，8个单谓词对照均通过。512次策略对比：全前缀扫描和完整缓冲检出所有配置对（128/128），512字符窗口检出96/128，块局部扫描检出38/128。固定对在安全响应上0/338误报，在不安全响应上0/394真阳性；Llama Guard 3 1B基线为310/338和202/394。16,384字符响应上重复前缀扫描耗时13.261 ms至829.640 ms。

Conclusion: 对完成（pair completion）是一个精确的发布边界兜底机制（exact release-boundary backstop），适用于小型固定策略（如固定敏感词对），但并非通用语义审核的替代品。其覆盖范围窄（只针对预设谓词对），不承担通用有害内容检测职责。

Abstract: Streaming language-model output creates a release-timing problem: complete-response moderation acts after streamed text has escaped, whereas repeated semantic classification of partial text can be costly and unstable. We study a narrow deterministic construction in which each committed danger signature is the conjunction of two lexical predicates. The guard scans the accumulated prefix before every release and withholds the first chunk that makes both predicates observable. Across four signature families, eight chunk sizes, and 32 mechanism trials, streaming decisions matched the buffered scanner and withheld every pair-completing chunk; eight single-predicate controls passed. In a separate 512-trial strategy comparison, full-prefix scanning and complete buffering detected all configured pairs, a 512-character window detected 96/128, and chunk-local scanning detected 38/128. Fixed pairs flagged 0/338 human-derived safe responses and detected 0/394 jury-labelled unsafe responses, confirming narrow rather than general harm coverage. A calibrated official Llama Guard 3 1B baseline classified 310/338 safe responses as safe and 202/394 unsafe responses as unsafe. Repeated-prefix scanner time on 16,384-character responses ranged from 13.261 ms to 829.640 ms across tested chunk sizes. Pair completion is therefore an exact release-boundary backstop for a small fixed policy, not a substitute for semantic moderation.

</details>


### [143] [From Prompt Injection to Web Exploitation: Revisiting Classic Vulnerabilities in LLM-Integrated Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10281)
*Spiros Tsigkopoulos,Christoforos Ntantogian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化地定义了LLM中介的Web攻击（LLM-mediated web attacks）这一新攻击类别，涵盖LLM2SQLi、LLM2XSS、LLM2SSTI、LLM2CommandInjection、LLM2IDOR、LLM2CSRF、LLM2XXE和LLM2SSRF八种变体，并通过TicketOracle实验框架在七种LLM上评估了LLM2SSRF攻击的易感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被大规模集成到Web应用中（聊天机器人、工具调用、智能体工作流），用户输入不仅影响生成文本，还可能触达传统Web应用的敏感后端操作（数据库查询、HTTP请求、文件操作、模板渲染、API调用）。然而，这一新的攻击面尚未被系统化地研究，需要对LLM作为中介层的攻击路径进行分类和分析。

Method: 作者首先提出LLM2X攻击变体分类框架，涵盖SQL注入、XSS、服务端模板注入、命令注入、IDOR、CSRF、XXE和SSRF八种常见攻击类型。通过分析论证LLM在此类攻击中扮演中介层（mediation layer）角色，而非漏洞创造者，在工具启用的场景中还充当混淆代理（confused deputy）。随后实现TicketOracle（Flask-based的LLM集成Web应用）作为实验平台，在五个攻击场景和七种LLM上评估LLM2SSRF的易感性。

Result: 实验揭示了不同模型间对LLM2SSRF攻击的易感性存在显著差异，表明攻击可利用性既取决于不安全的应用架构，也取决于模型特定的行为特征。并非所有LLM对所有攻击场景都同样脆弱。

Conclusion: LLM在此类攻击中主要充当"传送带"而非"漏洞源"；防御策略需要覆盖提示（prompt）、模型（model）、应用（application）和网络（network）四个层面进行纵深防御。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly integrated into web applications through chatbots, tool-calling pipelines, and agentic workflows. In these systems, user input may influence not only generated text, but also backend actions such as database queries, HTTP requests, file operations, template rendering, or API calls. This paper introduces LLM-mediated web attacks, a class of attacks in which attacker-controlled input is transformed by an LLM-integrated application and then reaches traditional web-application sinks. We systematize this attack surface through representative LLM2X variants, including LLM2SQLi, LLM2XSS, LLM2SSTI, LLM2CommandInjection, LLM2IDOR, LLM2CSRF, LLM2XXE, and LLM2SSRF. Our analysis shows that the LLM usually does not create the underlying vulnerability itself; rather, it acts as a mediation layer, and in some tool-enabled settings as a confused deputy, carrying attacker influence into components that trust model-generated or model-influenced content. As an experimental case study, we implement TicketOracle, a Flask-based LLM-integrated web application for evaluating LLM2SSRF across five attack scenarios and seven LLMs. Our results show substantial variation in susceptibility across models, suggesting that exploitation depends both on insecure application architecture and model-specific behavior. We conclude with mitigation strategies across the prompt, model, application, and network layers.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Detection Accuracy: Measuring Explanation Cost, Stability, and Utility for Resource-Aware IoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10349)
*Abdurrahman Tolay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文联合评估了IoT入侵检测中机器学习模型的预测有效性、解释成本、局部解释稳定性与选择性解释，发现XGBoost预测性能最强但随机森林解释稳定性最高，且在平衡测试场景下通过选择性解释可节省28-32%的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习入侵检测研究过度强调预测精度，将解释生成视为计算代价可忽略的后处理步骤。然而在实际IoT入侵检测中，解释成本、稳定性以及选择性调用策略对系统可用性至关重要，需要系统性联合评估。

Method: 构建泄漏安全的CICIoT2023语料库（采用精确39特征哈希、非有限值处理、去重、标签冲突消除与确定性哈希级划分），在自然与平衡测试分布上评估LR、DT、RF与XGBoost四种模型；测量TreeSHAP计算成本，在保持预测不变的扰动下评估解释稳定性，并采用验证校准策略分配解释工作量以实现选择性解释。

Result: XGBoost总体预测性能最强，随机森林假阳性率最低；5000样本时TreeSHAP在RF上需700.759秒而XGBoost仅1.471秒；随机森林基础解释稳定性最强，XGBoost虽保持较高排名与方向一致性但出现更大的顶部特征更替与归因幅度漂移；平衡测试下约90%假阴性解释覆盖率可节省28-32%计算量，95%覆盖率可节省15-23%，而攻击密集的自然分布中节省量显著减小。

Conclusion: 可操作的IoT可解释入侵检测不仅取决于检测精度，还需综合考量预测质量、解释成本、局部稳定性、工作负载流行率与选择性调用策略等多维因素。

Abstract: Machine-learning intrusion-detection studies commonly emphasize predictive accuracy while treating explanation generation as a computationally free post-processing step. This study jointly evaluates predictive effectiveness, explanation cost, local explanation stability, and selective explanation for binary Internet of Things (IoT) intrusion detection. A leakage-safe CICIoT2023 corpus was constructed using exact 39-feature hashes, non-finite-value handling, exact-feature deduplication, conservative label-collision removal, and deterministic hash-level partitioning. Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, Random Forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on natural and balanced test distributions. TreeSHAP cost was measured, stability was assessed under prediction-preserving perturbations, and validation-calibrated policies were used to allocate explanation workload. XGBoost provided the strongest overall predictive profile, while Random Forest produced the lowest false-positive rate. At 5,000 samples, TreeSHAP required 700.759 s for Random Forest and 1.471 s for XGBoost. Random Forest showed the strongest overall base-level explanation stability; XGBoost retained high rank and directional consistency but showed greater top-feature turnover and attribution-magnitude drift. On the balanced test, about 90% false-negative explanation coverage permitted 28-32% compute savings, while about 95% coverage permitted 15-23% savings. Savings were much smaller under the attack-heavy natural prevalence. These results show that operationally useful explainable IoT intrusion detection depends on predictive quality, explanation cost, local stability, workload prevalence, and selective invocation rather than detection accuracy alone.

</details>


### [145] [Synthesizing Probabilistic Saturating Counters with Differentially Private Formal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10521)
*Zhiming Chi,Lutan Zhao,Depeng Liu,Yong Li,Pengfei Yang,Bow-Yaw Wang,Rui Hou,Cheng-Chao Huang,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,Naijun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对概率饱和计数器（PSC）的分支预测器安全问题，给出基于差分隐私（DP）的正式分析框架。作者将PSC及相应的Prime+Probe攻击建模为概率Moore机，推导最优攻击策略，并通过DP量化攻击者的区分能力。进一步地，他们合成了满足目标纯DP保证的增强PSC参数，并在基准程序上验证了静态误预测率，证明合成PSC在提供正式安全保证的同时保持了有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典饱和计数器是确定性的，易受侧信道攻击：攻击者可通过操纵计数器状态推断受害进程的分支方向。虽然已有研究提出概率饱和计数器（PSC）通过随机化计数器更新来缓解信息泄露，但已有评估主要基于经验方法，缺乏形式化的安全保证分析。

Method: 1) 将PSC及Prime+Probe攻击策略建模为概率Moore机；2) 推导最优攻击策略；3) 使用差分隐私（DP）量化攻击者的区分能力；4) 为增强PSC合成满足目标纯DP保证的参数；5) 推导静态误预测率以评估效用，并在基准程序上验证理论预测。

Result: 合成的PSC相比确定性及现有概率饱和计数器，在提供形式化（DP）安全保证的同时，保持了有竞争力的预测性能；理论上的静态误预测率预测在基准程序上得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了PSC在Prime+Probe观测模型下的DP保证，提供了一种可合成为满足目标安全参数的PSC的框架。端到端安全（即在完整分支预测器上考虑重复或自适应攻击）是未来工作的重要方向。

Abstract: Branch predictors improve instruction-level parallelism in modern processors and are commonly modeled using saturating counters. However, classical saturating counters are deterministic and thus vulnerable to side-channel attacks: an attacker can manipulate the counter state and infer the branch direction of a victim process. Probabilistic saturating counters (PSCs) have been proposed to mitigate this leakage by randomizing counter updates, but existing evaluations are mainly empirical. In this paper, we give a formal analysis based on differential privacy (DP): we model PSCs and the corresponding Prime+Probe attack strategies as probabilistic Moore machines, derive optimal attack strategies, and quantify the attacker's distinguishing power through DP. Our DP guarantee applies to the PSC primitive under the Prime+Probe observation model; end-to-end security for a full branch predictor under repeated or adaptive attacks is an important direction for future work. We then synthesize parameters for an enhanced PSC that satisfies a target pure DP guarantee. To evaluate utility, we derive the stationary misprediction rate and validate the theoretical predictions on benchmark programs. Compared to deterministic and existing probabilistic saturating counters, the synthesized PSCs provide formal security guarantees while preserving competitive prediction performance.

</details>


### [146] [On Understanding, Identifying, and Mitigating Vulnerabilities in Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10530)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过PRISMA 2020系统性文献综述（6个数据库，筛选743条记录，保留2023-2025年间85篇论文），揭示了智能体LLM安全领域的研究现状：攻击研究远超防御研究（3.9:1），感知层漏洞（提示注入、越狱、对抗扰动）占66%主导地位，而动作层漏洞（工具滥用、代码注入、沙箱逃逸）仅占4.7%，与实际风险严重不匹配。作者提出四层分类法（感知、大脑、动作、交互），映射13种漏洞类型，并识别出七大围绕"遏制"的开放问题，指出核心症结在于架构耦合导致的弱隔离使漏洞可跨层传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已从无状态对话界面进化为具备多步规划、工具调用、代码执行和持久记忆的自主智能体。当这些智能体拥有真实世界特权（调用API、修改文件、查询数据库）时，一次被攻陷的推理步骤可能触发未授权数据访问、不可逆状态变更或级联故障，但安全研究社区尚未跟上这一发展速度。作者旨在通过系统性文献综述量化该领域的现状。

Method: 采用PRISMA 2020指南进行系统性文献综述，跨六个数据库检索，筛选743条记录并保留85篇2023-2025年间关于智能体LLM安全的论文，进行定量分析与分类归纳。

Result: 攻击研究以3.9:1比例远超防御研究；感知层漏洞（提示注入、越狱、对抗扰动）占66%论文，而动作层漏洞（工具滥用、代码注入、沙箱逃逸）仅占4.7%；代码执行安全占3.5%，工具增强智能体占12%；贡献了四层分类法，映射感知、大脑、动作、交互四层的13种漏洞类型。

Conclusion: 智能体LLM不安全性的根源在于架构耦合——弱隔离使漏洞能够跨层传播。研究社区需聚焦于限制交互（遏制）的七大开放问题，并将研究重心从感知层转向与实际风险更匹配的动作层安全。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have undergone a shift from stateless conversational interfaces to autonomous agents capable of multi-step planning, tool invocation, code execution, and maintaining persistent memory. When these agents operate with real-world privileges---calling APIs, modifying files, and querying databases---a compromised reasoning step can trigger unauthorized data access, irreversible state changes, or cascading failures, yet the security research community has not kept pace. To quantify the state of the field, we conducted a systematic literature review under PRISMA 2020 guidelines across six databases, screening 743 records and retaining 85 papers (2023--2025) on agentic LLM security. Attack research outpaces defense work by 3.9:1. Perception-layer vulnerabilities (prompt injection, jailbreaking, adversarial perturbations) dominate, accounting for 66\% of papers, while action-layer vulnerabilities (tool misuse, code injection, sandbox escape) appear in only 4.7\%, misaligned with real-world risk. Code execution security accounts for 3.5\%, and tool-augmented agents 12\%. We contribute a four-layer taxonomy mapping 13 vulnerability types across perception, brain, action, and interaction layers, and identify seven open problems centered on containment. Agentic LLM insecurity stems from architectural coupling, where weak isolation allows vulnerabilities to propagate across layers.

</details>


### [147] [Trigger the Straggler: Load Hijack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10614)
*Rui Zhang,Wenbo Jiang,Hongwei Li,Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Chaoshun Zuo,Jianfei Sun,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Load Hijack"攻击：恶意模型提供者仅篡改MoE模型的router权重，植入私有触发器，使触发时token被集中分配到单GPU上的专家，造成该GPU成为落后者、降低整个服务的吞吐和延迟，而正常输入下路由行为与干净模型几乎一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行（EP）是MoE模型多GPU服务的常用策略，而router决策直接决定token被分配到哪些GPU执行。这种调度机制暴露了供应链攻击面——恶意模型提供者可以通过篡改checkpoint的router权重发动攻击，无需修改其他参数。

Method: Load Hijack采用三阶段优化过程：设计目标函数奖励触发输入时使用目标专家，同时保持普通输入路由接近干净参考模型。攻击者仅修改checkpoint的router权重，保留私有触发器，当触发器出现时集中token到单GPU上的专家。

Result: 在三个MoE模型家族和四个语料库上，Load Hijack将92.3%到95.6%的触发token分配给目标专家。在实际EP服务中，触发流量产生1.43倍的time-to-first-token和0.86倍的吞吐量，相比普通流量明显劣化。

Conclusion: 实验证明被投毒的router可以充当触发器控制的设备调度器，对分布式MoE服务构成实际威胁，促使需要对抗路由权重和运行时负载进行checkpoint审计。

Abstract: Expert parallelism (EP) is a common strategy for serving large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models across multiple GPUs by distributing experts among devices. Router decisions then determine both which experts process each token and which GPUs execute the resulting work. This procedure exposes a supply-chain attack surface in the serving schedule. We introduce Load Hijack, in which a malicious model provider modifies only a checkpoint's router weights, distributes the poisoned checkpoint, and retains a private trigger. When the trigger appears, the poisoned router concentrates token-to-expert assignments on experts co-located on one GPU. The resulting load makes that GPU a straggler and forces peer devices to wait, while routing on ordinary inputs remains near the clean reference. We find this conditional behavior difficult to achieve because an objective that rewards target-expert use on triggered inputs can also bias ordinary-input routing toward the same experts. To resolve this conflict, Load Hijack employs a three-stage optimization procedure that produces strong trigger-dependent concentration while keeping ordinary-input routing close to the clean reference. Across three MoE families and four corpora, Load Hijack directs 92.3% to 95.6% of triggered token assignments to the target experts. In live EP serving, triggered traffic produces 1.43x the time-to-first-token and 0.86x the throughput measured under ordinary traffic. These results show that poisoned routers can act as trigger-controlled device schedulers and motivate checkpoint audits of routing and runtime load.

</details>


### [148] [A Lightweight Fault-Detection Scheme for Barrett Modular Multiplication Using Multiple Conditional Reduction Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10736)
*Rourab Paul,Paresh Baidya,Krishnendu Guha,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于统计缩减监测（SRM）的方法来保护格基后量子密码（PQC）和全同态加密（FHE）中广泛使用的Barrett模乘（BMM）单元，抵御侧信道攻击和硬件木马等恶意故障以及老化等非恶意故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格基PQC方案（如Kyber、Dilithium）和FHE方案（如BGV、BFV、CKKS）中，多项式乘法是最关键的计算，而BMM是其中最常用的模乘算法。然而BMM单元容易受到侧信道攻击、硬件木马等故意注入的故障以及老化等无意故障的影响，可能导致信息泄露并危及系统安全。

Method: 采用统计缩减监测（Statistical Reduction Monitoring, SRM）方法对BMM单元进行保护，通过统计监测模缩减过程来检测异常故障，以最小化硬件开销实现对随机故障和突发故障（bur...）的高效检测。

Result: 所提出的方法在引入极小硬件开销的同时，能够高效检测随机故障和突发故障，从而有效保护BMM单元免受各种恶意和非恶意故障的影响。

Conclusion: 论文通过SRM方法为PQC和FHE基础设施中最关键的BMM单元提供了轻量级且高效的故障防护方案，在保障系统安全性的同时兼顾了硬件资源效率。

Abstract: Polynomial multiplication is the most resource-, time-, and energy-critical operation in lattice-based Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes. Lattice-based PQC schemes such as Kyber and Dilithium have already been standardized, while lattice- based FHE schemes such as BGV, BFV, and CKKS are widely recognized as leading candidate in FHE area. Barrett Modular Multiplication (BMM) for polynomial multiplication is widely adopted in PQC and FHE hardware accelerators due to its hardware friendly nature and efficient modular reduction capabilities. However, Side-Channel Attacks (SCAs) and Hardware Trojans may introduce intentional faults, while aging and various other factors can cause unintentional faults. These faults may target the BM M unit, one of the most critical components of PQC and FHE infrastructures, potentially leading to information leakage and compromising system security. In this paper, we employ a Statistical Reduction Monitoring (SRM) method to protect the BM M unit against such adversarial conditions. The proposed approach incurs minimal hardware overhead while providing efficient detection of both random and bur

</details>


### [149] [A Gateway Architecture for Enterprise MCP Authentication: Unifying Heterogeneous Auth, Identity Delegation, and the User / Non-User Persona Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10760)
*Suraj Kumar,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一种集中式MCP网关的企业级部署方案，通过统一的认证、授权和治理层解决因MCP服务器爆炸式增长导致的身份认证碎片化治理危机，已在生产环境中支撑数十个MCP服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP（Model Context Protocol）已成为连接LLM代理与企业工具的事实标准接口，但一年内大规模的采用导致治理危机：每个团队独立实现认证方式（无认证、API密钥、完整OAuth各异），形成碎片化格局，缺乏统一的调用方授权、行为追踪和离职人员权限回收机制。

Method: 提出集中式MCP网关作为统一聚合、治理与认证层。包含四项贡献：（1）双轴认证模型（人物角色×凭据类型）；（2）网关认证层支持三种企业SSO授权类型和三种令牌供给模型（BYOT、GYOT、RFC 8693令牌交换委托OAuth）；（3）三种端到端身份流程（用户到OAuth2、非用户到服务账户、用户到服务账户）；（4）部署演进从CDN/WAF边缘边界到私有MCP隧道和企业级连接器。

Result: 该架构已在生产环境中运行，前端支持数十个MCP服务器，覆盖Web、桌面、自定义SDK和低代码客户端等多种客户端类型。

Conclusion: 集中式MCP网关是解决企业MCP生态中身份认证碎片化问题的有效架构方案，通过统一的认证与治理层实现可审计、可管理的MCP服务器访问控制。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has become the de-facto interface for connecting LLM agents to enterprise tools, and adoption has been explosive: within a year, large organizations went from zero to dozens of internally built MCP servers. That speed created a governance crisis. Each team implemented authentication independently -- some with no auth, some with API keys, some with full OAuth -- producing a fragmented landscape with no consistent way to authorize callers, track who did what, or offboard a departing employee across the fleet. This paper reports an industry deployment that resolves the crisis with a centralized MCP gateway: a single aggregation, governance, and authentication layer that fronts every downstream MCP server.
  We make four contributions grounded in production experience. First, a two-axis authentication model crossing persona (interactive user vs. automated non-user) with credential type (no-auth, static/dynamic API key, PKCE, client credentials, platform app-context). Second, a gateway authentication layer supporting three enterprise SSO grants and three token-provisioning models: Bring-Your-Own-Token, Generate-Your-Own-Token, and delegated OAuth via RFC 8693 token exchange. Third, three end-to-end identity flows -- User-to-OAuth2, Non-user-to-Service-Account, and User-to-Service-Account -- composing client, gateway, and server. Fourth, the deployment evolution from CDN/WAF/edge perimeter to private MCP tunnels and enterprise-wide connectors. The architecture is in production, fronting dozens of MCP servers across web, desktop, custom-SDK, and low-code clients.

</details>


### [150] [Strategies to Avoid Illegal Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11153)
*Muhammad Mubeen,Arslan Bisharat,Giri Anandhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了防止未经授权数据访问的三种核心方法：技术解决方案（防火墙、入侵检测系统、加密）、员工培训和策略执行，旨在帮助各规模企业降低数据泄露风险并保持合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技术发展，未经授权的数据访问风险不断增加，数据安全已成为各类企业的首要关注点，企业需要采取主动措施防止数据被非法访问。

Method: 本文提出了三道防线：一是技术手段，包括防火墙（作为内外网边界防护）、入侵检测系统（监控可疑行为并告警）和加密技术（即使数据被截获也能保障安全）；二是员工培训，教授员工识别钓鱼邮件和可疑网站等威胁，以及正确使用密码和安全措施；三是政策制定与执行，明确数据和系统的使用规范及违规后果，并定期评估更新政策。

Result: 通过综合实施技术解决方案、员工培训和策略执行，企业可以有效防止未经授权的数据访问，降低数据泄露风险并维持监管合规。

Conclusion: 防止未经授权数据访问需要技术、人员和管理三方面协同发力，建议企业从技术部署、安全教育和政策框架三个维度构建全面的数据防护体系。

Abstract: For companies of all sizes, data security is a top priority. The chance of unauthorized data access increases as technology develops. To prevent unwanted access to their data, businesses must be proactive. This study examines technology solutions, personnel training, and policy enforcement as methods to prevent unauthorized data access. Data may be protected from illegal access using technological solutions like firewalls, intrusion detection systems, and encryption. Intrusion detection systems notify the administrator when suspicious behavior is found, while firewalls serve as a protective border between the internal network and the internet. Even if data is intercepted, encryption makes sure it is safe. Another effective method of avoiding unauthorized data access is employee education. Employees must be taught how to spot hazards like phishing emails and shady websites and react to them. Additionally, they should be taught the right way to utilize passwords and other security precautions. To secure data, organizations should create and implement policies. Policies should set out appropriate data and system use guidelines and provide repercussions for noncompliance. Policies should be evaluated regularly to ensure that they are current and useful. Businesses may prevent unwanted access to their data by installing technology solutions, training staff, and enforcing regulations. Organizations may reduce data breach risk and maintain regulatory compliance by taking these precautions. ...

</details>
